26	function parseHeader(buf: Uint8Array): H {
27	  const r = new BinaryReaderLE(buf);
28	  const version = r.u32();
29	  if (version < 210) throw new Error(`version ${version} < 210 非 1.4 世界`);
30	  if (version >= 140) {
31	    r.bytes(7);      // magic "relogic"
32	    r.byte();        // fileType
33	    r.u32();         // revision
34	    r.u64();         // favorites
35	  }
36	  const sectionCount = r.i16();
37	  const pointers: number[] = [];
38	  for (let i = 0; i < sectionCount; i++) pointers.push(r.i32());
39	  r.seek(pointers[0]);
40	  const title = r.string();
41	  let seedText = '';
42	  if (version >= 179) {
43	    seedText = version === 179 ? String(r.i32()) : r.string();
44	    r.u64(); // worldGenVersion
45	  }
46	  if (version >= 181) r.bytes(16); // guid
47	  const worldId = r.i32();
48	  r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32(); // bounds
49	  const tilesHigh = r.i32();
50	  const tilesWide = r.i32();
51	  if (version >= 209) {
52	    r.i32(); // gameMode
53	    for (const g of [222, 227, 238, 239, 241, 249, 266, 267, 302]) if (version >= g) r.boolean();
54	  } else if (version === 208 || version >= 112) r.boolean();
55	  if (version >= 141) r.i64(); // creationtime
56	  if (version >= 284) r.i64(); // lastPlayed
57	  const moonType = r.u8();
58	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) r.i32();    // treeX
59	  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) r.i32();    // treeStyle
60	  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) r.i32();    // caveBackX
61	  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) r.i32();    // caveBackStyle
62	  r.i32(); r.i32(); r.i32();              // ice/jungle/hell back
63	  const spawnX = r.i32(), spawnY = r.i32();
64	  const groundLevel = r.f64(), rockLevel = r.f64();
65	  r.f64(); r.boolean(); r.i32(); r.boolean(); r.boolean(); // time/day/moonPhase/blood/eclipse

70	    spawnX, spawnY, groundLevel, rockLevel, dungeonX, dungeonY, isCrimson,

72	}

75	function findWorldIdDraw(seedInt: number, target: number, max = 600): number {
76	  const r = new UnifiedRandom(seedInt);
77	  for (let t = 1; t <= max; t++) {
78	    const raw = r.next();
79	    const candidate = Math.trunc(raw * SAMPLE_SCALE * INT_MAX);
80	    if (candidate === target) return t;
81	  }
82	  return -1;
83	}

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-19T01:00:59.830Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
// .wld 地图解析器（Terraria 1.4+，version >= 210）
// 格式参考 TEdit（Terraria-Map-Editor）的 World.FileV2.cs，此处为独立 TS 实现。
// 只支持读取：section header / header flags / tiles（RLE）/ chests / town npcs。
// bestiary 等尾部 section 通过 sectionPointers 跳过，不做 footer 校验。
import { BinaryReaderLE } from './BinaryReaderLE';

export interface WldTileData {
  active: Uint8Array;    // active 标志（dirt 的 id 是 0，必须用此数组区分空气）
  type: Uint16Array;
  frameX: Uint16Array;
  frameY: Uint16Array;
  wall: Uint16Array;
  liquid: Uint8Array;
  liquidType: Uint8Array; // 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜
  wire: Uint8Array;       // 电路位 bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动（红蓝绿=header2 0x02-0x08,其余=header3）
  half: Uint8Array;       // 半砖(WorldFile.cs:1519 header2>>4 & 0x7 == 1)
  slope: Uint8Array;      // 坡面 1-4(== 2-5;Tile.cs sTileHeader bits 12-14 编码)
  paint: Uint8Array;      // 方块油漆 0-31（WorldFile.cs:2571-2574 header3 0x08 位后随 1 字节）
  paintWall: Uint8Array;  // 墙油漆 0-31（WorldFile.cs:2583-2586 header3 0x10 位后随 1 字节）
}

export interface WldChest { x: number; y: number; name: string; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; }
export interface WldNPC { spriteId: number; name: string; x: number; y: number; homeX: number; homeY: number; }

export interface WldWorld {
  version: number;
  title: string;
  seed: string;
  tilesWide: number;
  tilesHigh: number;
  spawnX: number;
  spawnY: number;
  dungeonX: number;
  dungeonY: number;
  groundLevel: number;
  rockLevel: number;
  time: number;
  dayTime: boolean;
  /** 原版 header(WF:1252/1276-1278):月亮样式/月相/血月/日食 */
  moonType: number;
  moonPhase: number;
  bloodMoon: boolean;
  eclipse: boolean;
  isCrimson: boolean;
  hardMode: boolean;
  /** 原版 worldId（WF header；对话月相池等消费） */
  worldId: number;
  /** 原版洞穴背景四区样式（WF:1262-1268 caveBackStyle[4]） */
  caveBackStyle: number[];
  /** 冰雪/丛林/地狱背景样式（WF:1269-1271） */
  iceBackStyle: number;
  jungleBackStyle: number;
  hellBackStyle: number;
  /** 已砸暗影珠数（WF:1302 byte）——满 3 召 EoW/BoC 计数,随导入回填 */
  shadowOrbCount: number;
  /** 已砸祭坛数（WF:1303 int32）——新三矿轮换波次源/海盗入侵门 */
  altarCount: number;
  /** 困难矿世界锁定选种（WF:1315-1317 int32×3;-1 = 未砸过该档） */
  savedOreTiers: { cobalt: number; mythril: number; adamantite: number };
  /** 原版 gameMode（0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程；v<209 无此字段=0） */
  gameMode: number;
  /** 日晷/月晷快进四件套（WF:2120/2203/2449-2450；v<113 无 sundial 位=0、
   *  v<264 无月晷位=0/false——与 WorldFile 缺省分支一致） */
  sundialCooldown: number;
  /** 拜月教石碑重生倒计时（WF:2185-2192 int32，v108+；缺省 86400=CultistRitual.delayStart） */
  cultistDelay: number;
  fastForwardTimeToDawn: boolean;
  fastForwardTimeToDusk: boolean;
  moondialCooldown: number;
  treeX: number[];
  treeStyle: number[];
  treeTops: number[];
  tiles: WldTileData;
  chests: WldChest[];
  npcs: WldNPC[];
  /** 天气头字段（v>273 才有；更早版本晴天） */
  weather?: {
    raining: boolean; rainTime: number; maxRaining: number;
    cloudBGActive: number; numClouds: number; windSpeedTarget: number;
    slimeRainTime?: number;
  };
}

export class WldParseError extends Error {}

export function parseWld(buf: Uint8Array): WldWorld {
  const r = new BinaryReaderLE(buf);
  const version = r.u32();
  if (version < 210) {
    throw new WldParseError(`不支持的世界文件版本 ${version}（需要 1.4+，version >= 210）。请在游戏中重新保存该地图。`);
  }
  if (version >= 140) {
    // header magic（中文版为不同常量，这里宽放校验）
    const magic = new TextDecoder().decode(r.bytes(7));
    const fileType = r.byte();
    // TEdit FileType 枚举：None=0, Map=1, World=2, Player=3
    if (fileType !== 2) throw new WldParseError(`不是世界文件（fileType=${fileType}）`);
    if (magic !== 'relogic') {
      // 宽放：中文版魔数，只要不是明显非法就继续
      if (!/^[a-z]{7}$/i.test(magic)) throw new WldParseError(`无效的文件头魔数: "${magic}"`);
    }
    r.u32(); // revision
    r.u64(); // favorite flags
  }
  const sectionCount = r.i16();
  const pointers: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < sectionCount; i++) pointers.push(r.i32());
  // tileFrameImportant 位图（TEdit ReadBitArray：每字节 LSB 在前）
  const bitLen = r.i16();
  const frameImportant: boolean[] = new Array(bitLen);
  {
    let data = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < bitLen; i++) {
      if ((i & 7) === 0) data = r.byte();
      frameImportant[i] = ((data >> (i & 7)) & 1) !== 0;
    }
  }

  // ---- header flags ----
  r.seek(pointers[0]);

```

88	    const jt = ja ? (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999) : 0;
89	    if (vt !== jt) {
90	      r.dT++; mark(i);
91	      const k = `T${vt}>${jt}`;
92	      pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1);
93	      if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`T(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vt} j${jt}`);
94	    }
95	    if (c.w[i]! !== st.wall[i]!) { r.dW++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`W(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.w[i]} j${st.wall[i]}`); }
96	    if (c.l[i]! !== st.liquid[i]!) { r.dL++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`L(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.l[i]} j${st.liquid[i]}`); }
97	    if (c.hf[i]! !== st.half[i]!) { r.dHf++; mark(i); const k = `H${c.hf[i]}>${st.half[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`H(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.hf[i]} j${st.half[i]}`); }
98	    if (c.sl[i]! !== st.slope[i]!) { r.dSl++; mark(i); const k = `S${c.sl[i]}>${st.slope[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`S(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.sl[i]} j${st.slope[i]}`); }
99	    // lty：仅 liquid>0 的格裁决（vanilla 0 基 ↔ JS +1 基）
100	    const vl = c.l[i]! > 0 ? c.lt[i]! + 1 : 0;
101	    const jl = st.liquid[i]! > 0 ? st.liquidType[i]! : 0;
102	    if (vl !== jl) { r.dLt++; mark(i); const k = `Y${vl}>${jl}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`Y(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vl} j${jl} liq${c.l[i]}`); }
103	    const via = c.ia[i]! ? 1 : 0;
104	    const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;
105	    if (via !== jia) { r.dIa++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`I(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${via} j${jia}`); }
106	    // ghost（信息位）：非活性残留 type
107	    const vg = va ? 0 : c.t[i]!;
108	    const jg = ja ? 0 : (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999);
109	    if (vg !== jg) r.dGh++;
110	  }
111	  if (firstI >= 0) r.first = `(${firstI % W},${Math.floor(firstI / W)})`;
112	  r.pairs = [...pair.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([k, n]) => [k, n] as [string, number]);
113	  return r;
114	}
115	
116	const srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();
117	function labelOf(file: string, line: number): string {
118	  let src = srcCache.get(file);
119	  if (!src) { try { src = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n'); } catch { src = []; } srcCache.set(file, src); }
120	  for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {
121	    const m = src[l]!.match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\s]/);
122	    if (m) return m[1].trim();
123	  }
124	  return `${file.split('/').pop()}:${line}`;
125	}
126	
127	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('JS pipeline 8-channel slot diff vs /tmp/sw-slp/g (0..53)', async () => {
128	  for (const f of readdirSync(VG)) {
129	    const m = f.match(/^(\d{3})_(.+)\.typ$/);
130	    if (m) DUMPNAME.set(Number(m[1]), m[2]);
131	  }
132	  console.log(`dump names: ${DUMPNAME.size} (8ch dir=${G2})`);
133	  const rows: Row[] = [];
134	  let curStore: Parameters<typeof cmp>[2] | null = null;
135	  const seen = new Set<number>();
136	  const unmapped = new Set<string>();
137	  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
138	  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
139	  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
140	    if (curStore) {
141	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
142	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
143	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
144	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_mile8')) {
145	          const lab = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));
146	          const idx = LABEL2DUMP.get(lab);
147	          if (idx !== undefined && !seen.has(idx)) {
148	            seen.add(idx);
149	            rows.push(cmp(lab, idx, curStore!));
150	          } else if (idx === undefined && !unmapped.has(lab)) {
151	            unmapped.add(lab);
152	            console.log(`  [unmapped-label] ${lab} @${m[1].replace(/^.*?(src\/)/, '$1')}:${m[2]}`);
153	          }
154	          break;
155	        }
156	      }
157	    }
158	    return oReseed.call(this, s);
159	  };
160	  const t0 = Date.now();
161	  try {
162	    try {
163	      await generateWorld({
164	        width: W, height: H, seedText: SEED, evil: EVIL as -1 | 0 | 1,
165	        onWorldPartial: (w, i) => {
166	          if (!curStore) curStore = w.store as never as typeof curStore;
167	          const idx = SLOT_END.get(i);
168	          if (idx === undefined || seen.has(idx)) return;
169	          seen.add(idx);
170	          rows.push(cmp(`槽末#${i}`, idx, w.store as never as typeof curStore));
171	        },
172	      });
173	    } catch (e) { throw e; }
174	  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }
175	  rows.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);
176	  for (const r of rows) {
177	    const bad = r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa;
178	    console.log(`${String(r.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(r.idx) ?? '?'} ← ${r.tag.padEnd(22)} ` +
179	      `A=${String(r.dA).padStart(7)} T=${String(r.dT).padStart(7)} W=${String(r.dW).padStart(6)} L=${String(r.dL).padStart(6)} ` +
180	      `Hf=${String(r.dHf).padStart(6)} Sl=${String(r.dSl).padStart(6)} Lt=${String(r.dLt).padStart(6)} Ia=${String(r.dIa).padStart(4)} (Gh=${r.dGh}) ` +
181	      `${bad ? '✗ ' + r.first : '✓'}`);
182	    if (bad) {
183	      console.log(`     pairs: ${r.pairs.map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);
184	      console.log(`     samples: ${r.samples.join(' | ')}`);
185	    }
186	  }
187	  const firstBad = rows.find((r) => r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa);
188	  const firstGh = rows.find((r) => r.dGh);
189	  console.log(`\n★ 8通道首差边界: ${firstBad ? `#${String(firstBad.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstBad.idx)} @${firstBad.tag} ${firstBad.first} ${firstBad.pairs.slice(0, 4).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}` : '无（0..31 全绿）'}`);
190	  console.log(`★ ghost 首差边界(信息位): ${firstGh ? `#${firstGh.idx} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstGh.idx)} gh=${firstGh.dGh}` : '无'}`);
191	  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
192	  writeFileSync('/tmp/mile8.json', JSON.stringify(rows));

STATUS="$V86/gen86_status.tsv"
QUEUE="$1"
touch "$STATUS"

nsleep() {  # 分段 sleep,单段 ≤120s
  local total=$1 left=$1 chunk
  while (( left > 0 )); do
    chunk=$(( left > 120 ? 120 : left ))
    sleep $chunk
    left=$(( left - chunk ))
  done
}

guard() {  # 仅清 7794 端口残留,绝不触碰其他端口
  local lp
  lp=$(pgrep -f 'TerrariaServer.*-port 7794')
  if [ -n "${lp}" ]; then
    print "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] GUARD: leftover 7794 pid=$lp -> kill"
    /bin/kill -9 ${lp} 2>/dev/null
    sleep 3
  fi
}

gen() {
  local size=$1 seed=$2 name=$3 minwait=$4 evil=$5
  if [ -s "$V86/$name.wld" ]; then
    print "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] SKIP $name (exists)"
    return 0
  fi
  guard
  local log="$V86/log_$name.txt"
(eval):1: == not found

'/tmp/vserver86/s22222.wld';\nconst W = Number(process.env.SW_YS_W ?? 4200), H = Number(process.env.SW_YS_H ?? 1200), N = W * H;\n\nconst le16 = (p: string): Uint16Array => {\n  const b = readFileSync(p);\n  return new Uint16Array(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.length >> 1);\n};\n\n(RUN ? it : it.skip)('slot105 8ch vs vanilla wld', () => {\n  const pre = `${G2}/105_Final_Cleanup`;\n  const dt = le16(`${pre}.typ`), da = readFileSync(`${pre}.act`), dw = le16(`${pre}.wal`);\n  const dl = readFileSync(`${pre}.liq`), dhf = readFileSync(`${pre}.half`);\n  const dsl = readFileSync(`${pre}.slope`), dlt = readFileSync(`${pre}.lty`), dia = readFileSync(`${pre}.inact`);\n  const wld = parseWld(new Uint8Array(readFileSync(WLDP)));\n  const t = wld.tiles;\n  if (wld.tilesWide !== W || wld.tilesHigh !== H) throw new Error(`尺寸不符 ${wld.tilesWide}x${wld.tilesHigh}`);\n  console.log(`wld: seed=${wld.seed} evil=${wld.isCrimson ? 1 : dlt[i]! === 1 ? 2 : dlt[i]! === 2 ? 3 : -1;\n        if (vmap !== t.liquidType[i]!) { dLt++; if (!first.Lt) first.Lt = `(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${dlt[i]}(${vmap}) j${t.liquidType[i]}`; }\n      }\n    }\n    const via = dia[i]! ? - dungeonSide Right (+1?) → num7 = ((d*0.25+0.1)*(-1)+0.5)*W = (0.4 - d*0.25)*W → x ∈ [0.15W, 0.4W]
- dungeonSide Left (-1?) → num7 = ((d*0.25+0.1)+0.5)*W = (0.6+d*0.25)*W → x ∈ [0.6W, 0.85W]

```
while (true) {
    int num4 = (int)Main.rockLayer;
    int num5 = Main.maxTilesY - 600;
    if (num4 > num5 - 1) num4 = num5 - 1;
    int num6 = genRand.Next(num4, num5);
    int num7 = (int)(((genRand.NextDouble() * num3 + 0.1) * (-dungeonSide) + 0.5) * maxTilesX);
    if (remixWorldGen && !SecretSeed.noSurface.Enabled) { ... remix branch ... }
    else if (Main.tile[num7, num6].active() && Main.tile[num7, num6].type == 60)
    {
        flag = true; makeTemple(num7, num6, progress); break;
    }
    if (num2++ > 1000000) { ... }
}
```

JS:
```
for (;;) {
  let yTop = floor(rockLevel); yBot = h-600; if (yTop > yBot-1) yTop = yBot-1;
  const y = rng.int(yTop, yBot - 1);
  const x = floor(((rng.next() * band + 0.1) * -dungeonSide + 0.5) * w);
  if (st.inBounds(x, y)) {
    const i = st.idx(x, y);
    if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === JUNGLE_GRASS) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
  }
  if (fails++ > 1000000) {...}
}
```

So inBounds never matters.

2. JS: `if (fails++ > 1000000)` — vanilla `if (num2++ > 1000000)`. Same.

```
if (num2++ > 1000000) {
    if (num3 == 0.35) { num++; if (num > 10) break; }
    num3 = Math.Min(0.35, num3 + 0.05);
    num2 = 0;
}
```

const SANDSTONE_BRICK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_151_sandstone_brick']!;
const POT = TILE_BY_KEY['pot']!;
const SMALL185 = TILE_BY_KEY['v_185_small_decos']!;
const SAND = TILE_BY_KEY['sand']!;
const DUNGEON_WALLS = new Set([7, 8, 9, 94, 95, 96, 97, 98, 99]);

/** Pyramids pass（1456 cs:15426-15534）1:1：候选全部来自 DunesPass（GenVars.PyrX/PyrY）。
 *  校验链：x∈(300,w-300) → 地牢侧带回避（cs:15450-15452）→ 自 PyrY 下扫首个活性格
 *  须 < worldSurface 且为沙 53 → 与**全部先序候选**（含已被拒者）距 ≥220 → k-- 后 Pyramid()。
 *  无候选时直接无金字塔（原版路径如此；旧自掷兜底已删——非原版且白耗 RNG）。 */
export function runPyramidPass(
  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
): void {
  const accepted: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
  for (let idx = 0; idx < gs.pyramidSpots.length; idx++) {
    const cand = gs.pyramidSpots[idx];
    const num = cand.x;
    if (num <= 300 || num >= st.w - 300) continue;
    // 地牢侧回避（cs:15450-15452）：dungeonSide>Left 时须 num ≥ dungeonX+0.15w；
    // dungeonSide<Right 时须 num ≤ dungeonX-0.15w（任一不过即拒）。
    // ★dungeonX 此处 = generatingDungeonPositionX 的**终值**＝爬升入口走廊终点（入口 X，
    //   LegacyEntranceDungeonHall.cs:367 每条爬升走廊回写 genVars 覆盖 :394 的最高房
    //   中心 X 瞬态；Dungeon pass #36 先于本 pass），非 Reset 期 dungeonLocation。
    //   2026-08-16 审计修正：曾把 :394 瞬态当终值回填 topX。
    const dgX = gs.generatingDungeonPositionX || gs.dungeonLocation;
    const band = st.w * 0.15;
    if (!(gs.dungeonSide > -1 || !(num < dgX + band))) continue;
    if (!(gs.dungeonSide < 1 || !(num > dgX - band))) continue;
    let k = cand.y;
    while (k < st.h && st.inBounds(num, k) && !st.flags[st.idx(num, k)]) k++;
    if (k >= gs.worldSurface) continue;
    if (st.type[st.idx(num, k)] !== SAND) continue;   // cs:15455 沙列门禁
    // 与全部先序候选（PyrX[0..idx-1]）取最小距，须 ≥220（cs:15460-15472）
    let minDist = st.w;
    for (let l = 0; l < idx; l++) minDist = Math.min(minDist, Math.abs(num - gs.pyramidSpots[l].x));
    if (minDist >= 220) {
      if (PYR_TRACE) console.log(`PYR cand#${idx} x=${num} k=${k} minDist=${minDist} ACCEPT`);
      k--;
      pyramid(st, rng, gs, chests, num, k, 75, 125, false);
      accepted.push({ x: num, y: k });
    } else if (PYR_TRACE) {
      console.log(`PYR cand#${idx} x=${num} k=${k} minDist=${minDist} REJECT-dist`);
    }
  }
}

/** WorldGen.Pyramid（1456 cs:27816-28160）1:1：沙岩砖 151 主体 + 墙 34 +
 *  入口阶梯掏空 → 之字形下行隧道（端点房间：金箱 1 号主件 848/857/934 三选一、
 *  小堆 185×N、四支火把 91、整排瓦罐 28 style25-27）→ 长出口隧道。
 *  RNG 消费顺序逐掷对齐（num2/num3/num5/num8/num11/num12/房间段/出口段）。 */
function pyramid(
  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
  i: number, j: number, pyramidMinDepth: number, pyramidMaxDepth: number, noTunnel: boolean,
): boolean {
  if (PYR_TRACE) pyrRngCache = rng;
  const num = SANDSTONE_BRICK;   // 151
  const wall = 34;               // SandstoneBrickUnsafe
  const gi = st.idx(i, j);
  if (st.flags[gi] && (st.type[gi] === num || st.wall[gi] === 151)) return false;
  const active = (x: number, y: number) => st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
  const deact = (x: number, y: number) => { if (st.inBounds(x, y)) st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] = 0; };
  const setBrick = (x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
    const k = st.idx(x, y);
    st.type[k] = num; st.flags[k] = 1; st.half[k] = 0; st.slope[k] = 0;
  };
  const num2 = j - rng.int(0, 6);                       // j - Next(0,7)
  const num3 = rng.int(9, 12);                          // Next(9,13)
  let num4 = 1;
  const num5 = j + rng.int(pyramidMinDepth, pyramidMaxDepth - 1); // Next(75,125)
  if (PYR_TRACE) console.log(`PYR enter i=${i} j=${j} num2=${num2} num3=${num3} num5=${num5}`);
  // 主体：逐层加宽的实心沙岩砖塔（cs:27859-27868）
  for (let k = num2; k < num5; k++) {
    for (let l = i - num4; l < i + num4 - 1; l++) setBrick(l, k);
    num4++;
  }
  // 封闭区刷墙 34（cs:27870-27888：3×3 全砖）
  for (let m = i - num4 - 5; m <= i + num4 + 5; m++) {
    for (let n = j - 1; n <= num5 + 1; n++) {
      let all = true;
      for (let a = m - 1; a <= m + 1 && all; a++) {
        for (let b = n - 1; b <= n + 1; b++) {
          if (!st.inBounds(a, b) || !st.flags[st.idx(a, b)] || st.type[st.idx(a, b)] !== num) { all = false; break; }
        }
      }
      if (all && st.inBounds(m, n)) st.wall[st.idx(m, n)] = wall;
    }
  }
  let num8 = 1;
  if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) num8 = -1;                   // Next(2)==0 → -1
  let num9 = i - num3 * num8;
  let num10 = j + num3;
  const num11 = rng.int(5, 7);                          // Next(5,8)
  let flag2 = true;
  let num12 = rng.int(20, 29);                          // Next(20,30)
  if (PYR_TRACE) console.log(`PYR tunnel num8=${num8} num9=${num9} num10=${num10} num11=${num11} num12=${num12}`);
  let pyrIt = 0;
  // 入口掏空（cs:27903-27924）：自沙面斜向下切阶梯
  while (flag2) {
    if (PYR_TRACE && pyrIt < 400) console.log(`PYR stair it${pyrIt} num9=${num9} num10=${num10}`);
    pyrIt++;
    flag2 = false;
    let flag3 = false;
    for (let n13 = num10; n13 <= num10 + num11; n13++) {
      const n14 = num9;
      if (active(n14, n13 - 1) && st.type[st.idx(n14, n13 - 1)] === SAND) flag3 = true;
      if (active(n14, n13) && st.type[st.idx(n14, n13)] === num) {
        if (st.inBounds(n14, n13 + 1)) st.wall[st.idx(n14, n13 + 1)] = wall;
        if (st.inBounds(n14 + num8, n13)) st.wall[st.idx(n14 + num8, n13)] = wall;
        deact(n14, n13);
        flag2 = true;
      }
      if (flag3 && st.inBounds(n14, n13)) {
        const k = st.idx(n14, n13);
        st.type[k] = SAND; st.flags[k] = 1; st.half[k] = 0; st.slope[k] = 0;
      }
    }
    num9 -= num8;
  }
  num9 = i - num3 * num8;
  let flag4 = true;
  let flag5 = false;
  flag2 = true;
  // 之字形下行隧道 + 端点房间（cs:27928-28046）
  pyrIt = 0;
  while (flag2) {
    if (PYR_TRACE && pyrIt < 500) console.log(`PYR zig it${pyrIt} num9=${num9} num10=${num10} num12=${num12} f4=${flag4} f5=${flag5}`);
    pyrIt++;
    for (let n15 = num10; n15 <= num10 + num11; n15++) deact(num9, n15);
    num9 += num8;
    num10++;

#!/bin/zsh
# LLLLL batch: vanilla SmoothWorld action trace for seed 12345 (swtrace exe, x86).
# Protects the 9293480 swtrace.log archive via rename-restore (trap guaranteed).
set -u
ARCH=/tmp/sw-hsv/swtrace.log
ARCHBAK=/tmp/sw-hsv/swtrace-9293480-arch.log
MINE=/tmp/llll/swtrace12345.log
restore() {
  if [ -f "$ARCH" ] && [ ! -f "$MINE" ]; then mv "$ARCH" "$MINE"; fi
  if [ -f "$ARCHBAK" ]; then mv "$ARCHBAK" "$ARCH"; fi
  echo "[restore] done: arch=$(ls -la $ARCH 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $5}') mine=$(ls -la $MINE 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $5}')"
}
trap restore EXIT INT TERM
rm -f /tmp/llll/home/w.wld /tmp/llll/pc12345.txt "$ARCH" "$MINE"
if [ -f "$ARCH" ]; then mv "$ARCH" "$ARCHBAK"; fi
echo "[guard] archive moved: $(ls -la $ARCHBAK | awk '{print $5}') bytes"
cd /tmp/sw-hsv/Terraria.app/Contents/MacOS || exit 1
HOME=/tmp/llll/home SW_PASSCHAIN=/tmp/llll/pc12345.txt SW_HS_DUMP=1 SW_EVIL=1 \
  arch -x86_64 ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx -autocreate 1 -worldname w -seed 12345 \
    -world /tmp/llll/home/w.wld -port 7795 -noupnp -difficulty 0 \
    </dev/null > /tmp/llll/run12345.log 2>&1 &
SRV=$!

for i in $(seq 1 300); do
  sleep 1
  N=$(grep -c '^P|' /tmp/llll/pc12345.txt 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
  if grep -q "Server started" /tmp/llll/run12345.log 2>/dev/null; then echo "[poll] server started at ${i}s (passes=$N)"; break; fi
  if ! kill -0 $SRV 2>/dev/null; then echo "[poll] process exited at ${i}s (passes=$N)"; break; fi
done
sleep 2
kill $SRV 2>/dev/null; sleep 3; kill -9 $SRV 2>/dev/null
echo "[srv] killed"

/** SpiderCaves pass（L17455）1:1：w*0.005 个巢；采样 x∈[200,w-200) y∈[(ws+rock)/2,h-230)；
 *  countTiles 门 500≤空腔<3500（重试上限 w/2，超限放弃该巢；重掷 y 域换 rockLevel+30） */
export function runSpiderNestPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
  const __cap = (globalThis as { __swSpiderGsCap?: (gs: GenState, st: TileStore) => void }).__swSpiderGsCap;
  if (__cap) __cap(gs, st);
  // ★pass 头 tileSolid 翻转（cs:17459 `Main.tileSolid[379] = false`）：泡泡块非实心
  //   （countTiles 的 SolidTile 洪泛不再被 379 阻断）。本仓生成期不放置 379,行为无差,
  //   存档时间线——原版由 UndergroundJungleTrees 头（cs:17956,本仓缺该 pass,备案）还原。
  flipGenSolid([379], false);
  const __m = (globalThis as { __swSpiderNest?: (s: string) => void }).__swSpiderNest;
  const count = Math.floor(st.w * 0.005);
  const retryCap = Math.floor(st.w / 2);
  const yMin = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2);
  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
    let x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);
    let y = rng.int(yMin, st.h - 231);
    let c = countTiles(st, x, y);
    let tries = 0;
    while ((c.count >= MAX_TILE_COUNT || c.count < 500) && tries < retryCap) {
      tries++;
      // cs:17502-17504：重掷 x 仍 [200,w-200)，y 换 [rockLayer+30, h-230)
      x = rng.int(200, st.w - 201);
      y = rng.int(gs.rockLevel + 30, st.h - 231);
      c = countTiles(st, x, y);
      if (c.shroom > 1) c.count = 0;
    }
    __m?.(`NEST#${n} x=${x} y=${y} count=${c.count} shroom=${c.shroom} tries=${tries}`);
    if (tries < retryCap) spreadSpider(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y);
  }
  // ★pass 尾 tileSolid 翻转（cs:17521 `Main.tileSolid[162] = true`）：薄冰回实心
  //   （FragileIce cs:16783 曾翻非实心）——此后 UndergroundHouses 头（cs:17077）与
  //   LihzahrdTemplePart2 头（cs:17813）会再各自翻转。本仓 SpiderNest 槽在薄冰之后,
  //   时序与原版一致（FragileIce 16756 < SpiderCaves 17455）。
  flipGenSolid([162], true);
}

/** WebsInSpiderCavesAndHoney pass（L20140）1:1：
 *  墙86 蜂巢腔 → 液体转蜂蜜 + 1/3 钟乳石(165)；墙62 蜘蛛巢 → 清液体 +
 *  空格 9/10 放蛛网 51（±2-4 内有实心才放） */
export function runWebsInSpiderCavesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  for (let i = 100; i < st.w - 100; i++) {
    for (let j = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface); j < st.h - 100; j++) {
      const idx = st.idx(i, j);
      if (st.wall[idx] === 86) {
        if (st.liquid[idx] > 0) st.liquidType[idx] = 3; // honey
        if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) {
          // PlaceTight(spiders:false) → PlaceUncheckedStalactite（cs:38373-38402）：
          // 帧按上方实心块类型分派——蜂巢腔内上方必是蜂巢块 225 → 蜂蜜滴单格
          // fx=162+变体×18（162/180/198）、fy=72。此前错用雪地冰锥帧(0..36, 双格 fy 0/18)
          // ——蜂巢里挂出一串冰锥的根因
          if (solidTile(st, i, j - 1) && st.inBounds(i, j) && !st.flags[st.idx(i, j)]
            && st.inBounds(i, j + 1) && !st.flags[st.idx(i, j + 1)]) {
            const fx = 162 + rng.int(0, 2) * 18;
            st.setTileSilent(i, j, DECO165, fx, 72);
          }
        }
      }
      if (st.wall[idx] === 62) {
        st.liquid[idx] = 0; st.liquidType[idx] = 0;
      }
      if (st.wall[idx] === 62 && !st.flags[idx] && rng.int(0, 9) !== 0) {
        const r = rng.int(2, 4);
        let nearSolid = false;
        for (let k = i - r; k <= i + r && !nearSolid; k++) {
          for (let l = j - r; l <= j + r; l++) {
            if (solidTile(st, k, l)) { nearSolid = true; break; }
          }
        }
        if (nearSolid) st.setTileSilent(i, j, COBWEB, 0, 0);
      }
    }
  }
}

Version 1.4.5.6 Changes -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

I. Content & Additions
- Improved Russian language font.
- Improved Russian & Chinese localizations thanks to community feedback.
- Added a new hairstyle to character selection.
- Added Music Box (Rainbow Boulder) and Music Box (Silence).
- You can now place Bottles, Books, Clay Pots, and Candles on Metal Bars.
- Angler's chat window now only shows a notification near the quest button when a quest is available.
- Gems and Holiday Lights now have placement preview.
- Explosives' placement orientation can now be adjusted with the up/down keys.
- Digtoise is now placeable with Rubblemaker (Medium).
- Defender Medals now animate when used in shops.

II. Balance
- Soaring Insignia's unlimited flight now also applies to the Velociraptor mount.
- Gastropods and Eyezors no longer shoot faraway players.
- Enemies spawned from statues no longer pick up coins in higher difficulties.

III. Bug Fixes & Performance

'?'}\\t${cells.join('\\t')}`);\n}\nwriteFileSync('/tmp/panorama-yyyy.tsv', out.join('\\n'));\nconsole.log(`slots=${rows.size} → /tmp/panorama-yyyy.tsv`);\n// 摘要：每链首差边界 + 尾段总量\nfor (const [s] of chains) {\n  const rs = [...rows.entries()].map(([idx, m]) => ({ idx, r: m[s] })).filter((x) => x.r).sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);\n  const bad = rs.filter(({ r }) => chans.some((c) => r[c]));\n  const first = bad[0];\n  const last = rs[rs.length - 1].r;\n  const sum = chans.slice(0, 4).reduce((a, c) => a + last[c], 0);\n  console.log(`${s}: 首差 #${first.idx} ${DUMPNAME[first.idx]} | 终态(105) A=${last.dA} T=${last.dT} W=${last.dW} L=${last.dL} Hf=${last.dHf} Sl=${last.dSl} Lt=${last.dLt}`);\n}\nEOF\nnode /tmp/yy_panorama.mjs",
